ffor to make one pisse

Take Rewe and Parsley and Stampe them and Seethe them in
white wyne and Straine it, and put to it Sugar, and put it in
a glasse and drinke two or three tymes.

ffor the Strangarie

Seethe the under kynde of the younge branches of the Hasell
in small Ale vntill halfe be consumed, and drinke a good draught
therof and it is a present Remedye. Vse it till you be hole.

ffor them that cannot hold their water

Take Gootes Clawes and burne them in an Earthen pott all
to powlder and eate of the powlder in pottage. And it will helpe.

ffor one that pissethe blood.

Take Amerowes and Milefol of eyther in lieke quantitie and
Stampe them & temper them with Gotes mylke and drinke therof.

ffor to Restore mans Nature agayne lost by weomen

Take mylke of a Cowe that hath given mylke a longe tyme and t
in xij. Monethes hath had noe Calfe. and take alsoe as muche red
wyne and myngle them togither & drinke it and this will
searche every vayne. Alsoe it is good for bleeding beneathe./





ffor a Woman that hath lost hir flowres
This Medecyne never faylethe.

Take the Roote of Gladen which is lieke the flowre deluce and beareth a
yeallowe flowre, groweinge in drye Moores; and Seethe it in vinegre
or wyne, and when it is well sodden sett it on the ground, and make
hir stride over it, soe t noe Ayre goe from hir but to hir Privitie
This never faylethe. But take heede she be not wth Childe.

ffor a woman that hathe to muche of hir Flowres

Take the foote of a hare & burne it to powlder, and lett hir drinke of t
powlder wth Small Ale first and last.

ffor to Staye the ffluxe of flowres in weomen.

Take a quantitie of Sperma Cetti and dislove it in Redd wyne
vppon e fyre and lett the patient drincke it as warme as she
can suffer it in the Morninge & at night.

To make fflowres to goe.

Take Aresesmarte and Seethe it in Water till it be tender and
sett it vnder a hollowe Chayre; And lett the woman sitt theron
bare, soe close hir about with clothes that noe Ayre goe from hir but to &c

A Plaster for Vncleane fflowres.

Take Mynt, Verven, Mother woorte, Polipodie, vnsett Leekes,
Mallowes, Organu and wheete bren and Seethe them wth white wyne
and make a Plaster & laye it vnder hir Navell.

ffor Throwes in a Woman

Take vervayne sodden in water, and make Almond Milke
therof and lett the Sicke drinke it.

ffor a woman that lacketh Throwes in hir labor

Take iij Croppes of Neppe and as much of Bitteny and iij
Croppes of Redd Nettles, and Stampe it & Straine it wth
the drincke that shee drynkethe.

ffor e whites in a woman

Take a Lymmon and Slyce Rynd and all and lett it lye a daye &
a night in fayre runninge water, Soe much as may be dronke at
one draught. pved.





67
ffor the Mother.

Take ffrankencense, a Rynde of Cinnamome and a fewe Cloves,
and lett hir sitt in a close hollow Chayre wth Clothes close about
hir Legg. Then cast this poulder vppon a hott Tyle vnderneath
hir That the Smoke may ascend vpp into hir bodye. Then lett
hir stand and take Arsefeteda or burnt feather and hold at
hir nose.

An other for the Same.

Take the Gumm Lemney, And then take a peece of Scarlett
Clothe and cutt it round lieke a shillinge, and strike the
Gum on the Cloth, and laye it over hir Navell that
hath the mother and soe lett it lye till shea be well.

ffor the bloodie flux a good Medycyne.

Goe to any Lyme Kill and in a Lyme Stone you shall have a rounde
ball lieke an Egge wch is called a flynt Egg breake it and therin
you shall have a kynd of flowre which flowre you shall put into stronge
Ale or Mylke Sodden and drinke it. pved.

ffor to Stopp any Laxe.

Take Redd wyne and put to it Cynamome in fyne powlder halfe
an ounce, of Bolearmoniake iij. penny weight, of fyne Chalke in
poulder halfe an ounce. and temper them altogither with a little Sugar
and blood warme drinke it. and lett him not drinke in Tenn howres after
Nor lett him eate any Butter. Alsoe Seethe gum Arobake in fyne
powlder in redd Rose waterand drincke it wth the wyne.

To staye the fluxe.

Take iiij. Nuttmegg pricked full of holes, and steeepe them in vinegre
one night. Then take them and rost them in Embers and beate
them small to poulder and drinke of this poulder in Redd wyne
everye morninge fastinge







ffor to Stopp a Sore Laxe.

Take the Ryne of a Pomgranade and Stampe it to poulder
and put to it as much Chalke wth a halfpenny weight of
Cynamon. And drinke it wth good stronge Ale blood
warme. This hath bene pved diverse tymes by great men.

ffor the fluxe.

Take Beae wax and Seethe it in Mylke till it be melted and
let the partie greeved eate it downe as hott as he can suffer
it. And in twise eateinge of it, it will helpe him.

ffor a Laxe or dissentarie an approved good medecyne.

Taye Reysons of the Sonne and pull out the Stones of them
and rubb the Stones drye wth a Clothe, and then beate them
to powlder in a Morter, and let the patient take a good Spoone-
full of the powder at a tyme and wth beare or Ale let him
drinke it downe, and he may Staye the Laxe as he will
him selfe wthout any danger. It beinge taken iiij. or v.
times in this manner will Staye a verie greate loosnes.
diverse tymes pved.

ffor the Runninge of the Raynes pved.

Take two knockles of Mutton, gashe them well with a knife,
and breake well the bones and Seethe them iij. howres in iij.
Quart of newe mylke with these herbes in it: viz. Comfarie,
Sheaperdes purse, knott grasse and Planten of eache a
handfull. and in the Seethinge skum awaye the fatt,
that e mylke may be the clearer let yo herbes alsoe be Cutt thoghr not very small, and lett the patient drinke
therof Eveninge and Morninge, till he be hole. This hath
holpen diverse. If you keepe your herbes all the yeare gather
them at Mydsomer and drye them in the Shaddowe, and they
will keepe in vertue all the yeare & the better pved.






68
ffor weomens sore Brest not broken

Take Sauge and Margold that beare the black Seedes and Nettles
of the Sharpest of eache a handfull, and bruse them altogither,
and soe laye it to the brest and it will keepe it from breakeinge.

ffor a womans Sore breats brolen or not broken.

Take Oyle of Roses, Beane fflowre e youlk of an Egg &
a little vinegre and temper them altogither, and sett them vppo
Sout Embers, as it may be Skant Lue warme And then wth
a feather strike it vppon the Sore Breast Eveninge and morninge
and at Noone. Vse this and it will helpe.

ffor a Sore Brest.

Take a pottle of Runninge water and a greate handfull of
Sauge, and let it seethe to a quarte. Then take the liquor & put
therin a pynte of white Vinegre, and three Spoonefull of
goood honey, and Seethe all these vppon the fyre e Space of
halfe an howre Then dipp a Skarlett Clothe in the liquor
and laye it close about the Brest. and vse it soe three or
fowre tymes in a daye alwayes warme. And it will
helpe. pved diverse tymes.

An approved good Medicyne for the Stone

Take white wyne and make a possett, take younge elder
of a yeares growethe and take the inner barke and
stampe it and strayne it into the wyne before the
milke be put to it, and drincke as much of it as he
can. eveninge and morninge nyne tymes dayes
togither in the fall of the leafe or in the springe.
and walke vppon it and it will disolue the stone
in the bladder. make it not at the first to stronge

Take a Hare alive and strangle it and then only take of the Skin and put it in whole
into the Oven and drye it till you may beate it into powlder and then drinke of the
same in white wyne or beare, and this is an approved good medycyne for the Stone.





Diverse kynds of Laxatives. Necessarie
bothe for men & weomen

Take Skamone, Rubarbe, Sugar Candie quantitie for quantitie
but more of Rubarbe then of any of the other. to the value of
all Sixe pence halfepenny. viz. Skamonie ijd Rubarbe iijd
Sugar Candie ob. Pound all these togither, and drinke it
with a little quantitie of wyne or Ale.

Memorandum. He that taketh this Laxative or any other
that is dangerous: Must take it 3. dayes before the Change
or 3. dayes after. 3.dayes before the pryme or 3. dayes after
3. dayes before the full or 3. dayes after the full. probatum.

A Purgation clled Diacredij./

Take of honey a quarte and of Sugar a .li. and Seethe them till
you can make a droppe to stand on yo finger, lieke frost congeled,
vppon e howse Eaves. Then take it of, an in the Coolinge
put to it 2. . of poulder of Skamonie, and cast it on e
cold borde. and when it is cold cutt it in Losenges and put
it vpp. for it is a Stronge Laxative for him t can beare it

A Laxative for the Collick Substantially
and many tymes pved verie good

Take Clare and Nepp. Stampe them and Straine them
thorough a Clothe, and make a possett of the strongest
Ale you can gett. and looke that you have as muche Iuyce in
a manner as Ale but not fully soe muche. And before
you cast yo Mylke to make yo possett See you have a quan-
titie of good freshe butter that never Salt came in nor
washed wth water, and make the possett of all these
and as hott as the patient can drinke it, give it when
the fervent Collick comes vppon him: and Cover him soe
hott vpon a Bedd that he may Sweate, and by the grace
of God he shalbe well pved.

An Easie Laxative

Take Sene & Alesander of eache an ounce, of ffygg and Licorie.
halfe an ounce and Seethe them in a quart of good Ale and put to
it an ounce of Sugar Candye, and Skum it Cleane and straine
it. probatum.

An other Easie Laxative./

Take three Cornes of Spurge of Ginger & Sugar of each a
dragme and make therof a poulder and eate therof in the
Morninge an howre or two before you ryse. and vse this twise
Et Sanabis.

An other Laxative.

Take 3. Cloves, and to everye Clove 3. graines and to everye
graine a Corne of Spurge, and meddle them altogither and
take noe more at a tyme then will Cover a pennye.

An other Laxative

Take halfe an ounce of the powlder of the Root of Polepodie
of the Oke and as much of Sene in poulder. and take five
ounces of Honey and Seethe it. and put to it the poulder of
the Polepodie Seene & Aniseseed Lycoris and Sweete fennel
seede in pouldewr, and lett it Seethe till it be thick. and make
rownde ball therof and eate one or two of them at a tyme
fastinge./

A Suppositorie Laxative./

Take Camamell, Mallowes & Myntes wth a good quantitie
of Maij butter and a great handfull of graye Salt and
Seethe all these togither in a good quantitie of runninge
water to a pynte and with a Pype give this Suppositorie
Et fiat./

An other good Laxative

Take Succorie, Endive violett leaves strawberry leaves, a fennell & a parsley
roote with the pith taken out Rayson of the Sonne and figges.

clearesr of the water some 3. or 4. spoonefull and put in it the wayt
of 2d in Sene, and the weight of 6d in Aniseede and the weight of 4.

ffor the Sciatica in the Hipp

Take a Tenche, Cutt of his head and Tayle, and take out all
the bones, Then Sprinkle it wth Cummin Seede beaten into
fyne poulder. And applye it to the Sore hipp at night goeing
to bedd and in the Morneinge take it awaye.

An Excellent Remedy for the Gowte
ffor Ache and payne in the Ioynt.

Take Mylke of a Colored Cowe and wheate meale
and meddle them together and Seethe them till they be
thicke, and stirr it well, Then cast in the Iuyce of Ache
or Smalledge. Then Spread it plaster wise on a
Clothe and laye it warme on the Knee or Ioynte.

A pure Medecyne for the Gowte.

Take ijli of Virgin Waxe a li of posett a qrter of Stone
Pitche made in poulder, halfe a li of Rosen, as muche
of deere Suett, & a qrt of Cloves as much of Mace xijd
weight of Saffron, And put them in a Vessell of two
Gallons over a Sharpe fyre, wth a quart of good Redd
wyne & as muche of white wyne, Then put in yo wex
cutt in small peeces, yo perosett, Stone Pitche and yo
Rosen, and when it almost consumed, then put in yo deere
Suett, and let them boyle well togither, and stirr it
well wth a Slyce for cleaveinge to the pann side.
Then take an other pann and washe it wth fayre warme water
and then take yo liquor & straine it into t pann & sett it over
the fyre agayne & lett it boyle a little longer. then take it from
the fyre. then take Cloves and Mace well beaten and searsed
in a fine Searse and as it Coolethe cast in yo Spyces, and
when it wexeth cold, then cast in yo Saffron, small grownd as
is possible, And when it is almost cold take it vpp and put it in
a Boxe or Boxes. And when the Gowte is come take and make
a Plaster of it to e patient. and it will heale him. pved.

ffor the hott Gowte

Take Hearbe Grace and Cellebdyne of eache a handfull
and three Spoonefull of blacke Soape. pound it and straine
it thoroughe a Clothe and annoynte it wth a feather and
Cover it with tewed Sheepes leather.

ffor the Colde Gowte.

Take a pynte of good Ale clarefied over the fyre, put therto
a handfull of Baye Salt, and stirr it well a good whyle. Then
put therto two Spoonefull of blacke Soape and beinge sodd
togither annoynte the patient. But first rubb him with a linen
Clothe halfe an hower.

ffor the Gowte.

Take a quarte of newe Mylke, and put therto the donge of a Cowe or Oxe
and seethe them togither till they be thick and warme laye it to e place
wher the Gowte is.

them on a lynned Clothe, and Strowe on it some Sanguis dra-
conis beate into Small powlder. and laye it to e Sore viij. Dayes vnremoved

Philosofers and Doctors wch by their learninge studied many
things yt belonge to Physique & Chirurgerie, by the wch they have
well approved by their Scyence; That the Hearbes, frutes and Flowers
that be growinge uppon the Earthe are Comfortable and Mederynable
for mens bodyes: For lieke as ther are fowre Complexions in mens
bodyes that is to saye: Sanguine Complexion wch is hott & moiste,
Flegmatique Cold & Moiste, Colerique hott and drye, Melancoli=
que Cold and drye: Soe they fynd by their learninge that
Hearbes groweinge uppon the Earthe have the fowre Complexions
Wherfore they have devised and wrought and committed to
Writeninge the Natures and kynds of diverse hearbes to
helpe and Comfort men in tyme of Sicknes.

Absythium
Absynthium is an Hearbe in Englishe called wormewod, an Herbe
soe commonly knowne that it needeth noe description of this read pline.
It hath many Vertues. It is Laxative. It comforteth the Stomake
It Clensethe the Harte. Neverthelesse sometymes this Hearbe hindreth
digestion and hurtethe the Stomake if it be not rightlye used.
It must therfore be sodden in Rayne Water and it must stand all night
wth the mouthe of the vessell open in some cleane place that the ayre
may come into it And in the morninge drinke a quantitie therof
fastinge and abstayne till noone, and then eate freshe in eate, and
use this three dayes and it will comfort the Stomake, and clense it
of Coller, Glutt and Fleayme. It will asswage the Swellinge of ye
Wombe and kill wormes therin. It will purge flowres that have bene
longe in a womans bodye, if it be mynistered as a Suppositorie wth a little
Woolle. And it will put out an Abortive. Alsoe take Cereporontayne, Spikmard
oyle oliffe & boyle it well wth Aysell & the Juyce of wormewoode, then straine it
and drinke it and it will put awaye all wilbe about ye harte. It is called Can
dura, and of the Liver yt is called Apatica. The Juyce dronke is good for the
Jaundies. The leaves therof tempered wth Aysell is good for ye mydriffe. It is
good to put awaye dronkennes and tempered wth honey it is good to put awaye Swellinge in ye
mouthe if it be drinke, and it putteth awaye ye blacknes about the eyes & clensethe ye sigetalfe
ye Juyce on ye water still & put into ye Nostrille dryethe ye moystnes of the head. The Juyce mixed
wth ye gall of a bull beinge put in his eare yt hath noyse in his heade will helpe him presently.
dry it & lay it amongst Clothes & it will destroye moathes. and laye it on a sicken limb
head eare he know it & it will make him sleepe. The Juyce will kill mytes in ye head if it be washed
therwth. The Nature of this hearbe is hott & drye lieke unto fyre & hath two Spices. To have
the Juyce, gather it in May. To keepe, when ye flowres Springe.





Abrotanum. Sothernwoode.

Abrotanum is an Hearbe called Sothernwoode in Englishe.

The vertues an properties therof.

The Seede therof brayed and drunke with water healethe the Stone and the
Palseye. And drinke it wth wyne, and it helpethe the byteinge of
Venemous beast. The powlder of this hearbe medled wth Barley
Meale vnbyndethe hard Apostemes. And drye it &
meddle it wth wooll Oyle and annoynte the place that lackethe Hare
and it will make it growe agayne. Alsoe Seeethe it and vse it. ffor it
helpethe the Synnewes & the Breste & it destroyethe the ytching of e fleshe
Drincke it with wyne & it helpethe the Raynes of the backe and the
paynes of woomens Secrett. It closethe the flowers. It clenseth e brest
and It makethe good vrine. The Savor therof drivethe Snakes
Adders and other Venemous beast out of e place wher it is. Alsoe
seethe it in water and drinke it & it helpethe e cold fevor. Or else
Seethe it in Oyle and therwth annoynte the bodye before the ffevor comethe.
The Iuyce therof drunke sleaethe wormes in the bodye. Alsoe
Seethe it in water with Cromes of white bread & wth a fructe that is
called Malacidonia & it dothe awaye a Sicknes called Empatickes
And Stampe it wth Boores grease or with Tallowe and make a plaster
therwth and it will drawe out Thornes or Slivers out of the fleshe.
and if it be dronke wth wyne or layd privilie vnder a mans bedd
head it encouragethe him to Venerie. And t drinke keepethe a
man from Speakeinge in his Sleepe.

The nature of Sothernwoode

It is hott and drye in the third degree of e nature of e fyre.

Acedula. Sowrdock in English.

The vertues and properties therof.

Eate in Somer and it maketh a good stomake. Stampe it and laye it to the Belley
that fretethe, to the hott Podagra the burneinge gowte, to the Swelling
Eyes and it healethe m all. Alsoe e greene leaves boyled in oyle oliffe healeth
the old headache if it be applyed therto. And drinke it wth wyne and it killeth
wormes in e bodye, It restrayneth the flux. It helpeth him that hath dronke
Venome. The Iuyce therof clearethe e sight; Alsoe he that beareth this herbe
about him shall not be bitten wth Serpents nor wth madd Dogg, yf one hath
his Eyes closed wth Rhume or gummed take the iuyce therof and annoynte
the Eye lidd therwth & it helpeth them. This herbe is cold and drye
in e third degree. and is of the nature of the Earthe.





Asphodillus   Lege Plmium.
Asphodell or Cenchef or  Centum Capita is an hearbe the leaves wherof
be lieke Leekes & bearethe a yeallow flowre and round Codds, the Seede
is lieke Oynion Seede.

The vertues and properties of Asphodell.
The Branches Stamped and droken heale the dropsy Also the
leaves & the flowres dronke wth wyne healeth the byteinge of veno
mous beasts and wormes. The juyce wth the Roote a little  Ivrie &
Saffron sodden togither wth old sweete wyne cleane strained is good to
heale runninge Eyes. Alsoe the Roote beaten to poulder meddled wth
a little honney and annoynte the place therwth that lacketh haire
and it will make it growe agayne. Alsoe take the Juyce therof
and wett a Clothe therin and rubb the Morsewe therwth and it will
have it awaye.

The Nature therof hott & drye of ye Nature of Fyre.
Agnus Castus
Agnus Castus us an hearbe called Futtisane or Parkeleaves. This
hearbe hathe leaves wth Synnen & lieke Planten and bearethe a
yeallow flowre wth black berryes when they be rype and growethe
in wooddye and drye places.

The vertues and properties of Agnus Castus
Phylosofers affirme that the Juyce and Seede of this hearbe as it may
be used helpeth Chastitie. It openethe the powers of the bodye and
letteth out wvill humors, and dryeth mans seede. Seethe it wth Fennell
seede & Aysell and it is good to destroye the cold dropsy. Alsoe Seethe
this hearbe wth Smallage and Sauge in water & Salt and bynd it to
the hinder parte of the head and it helpethe the Lyturgie. And
eate it rawe and it engendrethe the headache. And lett man or
woman bare it about them wakeinge or Sleepeinge and it will
quenche the Lust of Vendrie. And it destroyethe hardnes & stopping
of the liver & the Melt.

The nature of it is hott & drye.

Agrimonie otherwise called Eupatoria of R: Eupator.
It is described in Plinie
It hathe leaves lieke Tinsey but greener and a yeallow flowre, the
Seeds are lieke a beard brushe & will hange on ones hose; The properties
If it be eaten greene wth the Roots it helpethe ye akeinge of the Stomake
& wombe. the ooulder therof dothe the lieke effect. And tempered wth
Aysell it is good for a Sore that is hurt wth fyre Seethe it wth meate
& it is good for ye ache and ye Sore of the Melt
It is hott & drye.





Vide Plinium Alesander or Stanmarche Hipposelinu grace

Alexander or Stanmarche soe called is a common hearbe
It hath blacke Seed wthout flowres. The Properties & vertue
Put the leaves therof in Pottage and it will destroye all
manner of Apostemes.And the Seede therof Eaten every
daye destroyeth the Stone and helpeth the tremblinge Palsey.
It cureth the wringe and Tormentes of the Gutt. The same
beinge boyled & dronke with honied wyne helpeth the Stranguary
and difficultie of makeinge water. The roote sodden in wyne
expelleth Stone and gravell by vryne. It puteth awaye alsoe the
paine of e loynes flankes and sides. Taken in drinke or
applied as a lyniment it healeth the bytinge of a madd dogg.
The iuyce therof drinke heatethe them that are reddye to Chill and
be frozen wth cold./

1. Mountaine Ach or
Persely of the hill.

And as for the garden
Ach comonly called
persely ther be many
kind therof.

Ach hath these Properties

It is a good hearbe for Sore Eyes, Sore Pappes, and for the
heate & burninge of the Stomake. The Iuyce medled wth
the Cromes of white bread made in a plaster swagethe
the Swellinge & akeinge of the Eyes. The Root sodden &
dronke are good for the Coughe. It encreaseth mylke in a
womans breast It destroyeth venome. Seethe it in water
& drinke it and it ceaseth castinge. Vse to eate Ache fasting
Rawe and it will make one well coulored and it destroyethe
the cotidian fevor. Drinke Ache and fennell togither & it
destroyethe the dropsey. It helpeth the Spleene, and puteth awaye
freckles. Alsoe a Plaster made of Ach, Egg and dust of
a Myll in lieke quantitie, helpeth evill healed wound & sore
Savored.  This hearbe is hott and drye.

Gladiolus. Acorus. Gladiatus. Pettie Gladen. Flags or Swordwort

Acoras is an hearbe called Gladen. The vertue and propertie.
The Roote Sodden in Wyne and dronke helpeth the Coughe, and those that
be Invenomed & It healethe the Lymmes that are Shronke wth Cold.
The Roote therof put into a wound. Stancheth the bleedinge therof.
It is hott and drye.






73
Alleluya. Oxis. Cuckowes meate woodsorrell or Sorrell de Boise.
or woodsowre. or Stokeworte.

Alleluya is an Hearbe that groweth in Woodes. and hath three leaves
theron foldinge downeward and a little white flowre.

The vertues.

This hearbe yf it be wrapped in the leaves of the Redd docke and rosted in
Embers and laïd to a wound or Sore. It will frett out all the deade
fleshe therin. It will destroye wartes & Cornes. It is singuler good
to be given to one that hath lost his Stomake and appetite to his
meate. They alsoe that have a Rupture and their Gutt fallen
downe eate therof to verie good vse & successe. Also gather a good
quantitie of this hearbe with the Rootes. Stampe it wringe out the Iuyce
into a cleane Vessell, then take the Clearest from the drosse,
and it will take out yron Mouldes out of Clothe, layeinge them
on a fayre Clothe in Sommer and wth a feather annoynt the Iron
Mould often therwith, and as it dryeth in still annoynte it vntill
it be as white as the rest of the Clothe. It is hott and drye./

Allium. Garlicke. Lege Plinium lib:9. Cap. b.

Allium est Triaculum Rusticorum called Garlicke.

The vertues and Properties.

It Vnbindethe Stoping and Evill wynd in a mans bodie and
maketh good wynd, But it is contagious for the Sight, It healethe
the byteinge and stinginge of Adders. Noe venemous beast will touche a
man t annoynteth him self wth the iuyce therof. The iuyce myngled
with Honey is good for byteinge of Hownd. for bruses, hartm payne in the
bodye and Bladder. Alsoe lett Garlick be layd in a Pann of Coles
and let a woman stryde over it, that the Ayre may ascend vp into
hir Secrett and it will bringe fourthe the second burthen of hir
child. If it be eaten Rawe or sodden it will clense e lung and cleare
the voyce. Seethe it with Centaurie and it healethe the dropsie, and
dryethe evill humors of the boddye. and it helpethe franticke
men and the fallinge Sicknes. Drinke it wth Coriander and
wyne and it helpethe the Iaundise Seethe it wth Beanes and
rubb the Temples therwth & it is good for the head ache.Alsoe Seathe
it wth fatt Porke in growle and it is holsome for the wombe Braye
it wth Swynes grease and it asswageth Swellinge. and seethe
it in Sweete Mylke and vinegre and it killeth wormes in the
bodye. And whosoe vse to eate first therof every daye. It
will keepe him from evill ayre & from the Pestilence. It is
hott & drye.







Althaea Holy-hocke. de Altheaea lege Pliniu lib.20. Cap. 21

Althaea is an Hearbe called Holyhock and wyld Mallowe. The properties
If it be Shredd wth Sheepes Suett and layd to the Podagra it will
heale it in three dayes. If it be sodden in Vinegre & lynseede
and layd to a mans side it will drive out all evill wynd and
evill gathering togither in a mans bodye.
It is Temperate betweene hott and drye./

Amarusca

Amarusca is an hearbe called Maythe or Mayd weede or dogg ffennell
It is like to Cammamell and it very common.
The Iuyce therof Dronke healethe the Canker. The Pipes of the
Emerodes. Therbe two Species of them. One beareth a white
flowre and the other a Redd, and it groweth to common amongst
Corne. Annoynte the Squinantie with the iuyce of this hearbe
two or three dayes agaynst a hott fyre and it will helpe it in
short tyme. Stampe the Stalke of this hearbe and all and give
it him to drinke that hath lost his Speach Soddenly by Sicknes
or otherwise and it will make him speake agayne in short tyme.

Amees.

Amees is an hearbe called Ameo, Wood nepp or Penyworte
It hath leaves like Elder but they are lesser, And hath a
longe branche & it hath flowres and seed lieke Perselie

The Properties

If it be dronke wth honey it killeth wormes. It heateth the
Stomake t is cold. The liver and the Reynes. and it helpeth
the byteinge of venemous beastes. It provoketh vryne t
is wthholden by grosse humors. It loosnethe greate wynd
It breaketh the Stone in the Bladder It purgethe the
Raynes & Matryce if it be dronke wth Honey.
It is hott and drye./

Anabula Centemalles wyld Spurge

Anabula is an hearbe called Centemalles or wylde Spurge It groweth vppon
a plaine stalke, and beareth noe leafe but at e top, and ther it groweth broade
& of rownde compasse & when it is broken e iuyce is lieke mylke.

The vertues and Properties

Take it when it is younge and old Barrowes grease and boyle them in white
wyne vntill e wyne be nighe consumed then let it Coole a while then put
therto quick Silver that is killed wth fastinge Spettle & Ashes Stirr them
well togither and everie daye annoynte the drye Scabb therwth and it
will heale it. The mylke therof will kill Wart. And is good for e Toothe ache





74
Auancia

Auancia is an hearbe called Hares foote It bearethe a yeallowe
flowre, The Propertie and vertue.

If it be dryed & dronke in powlder wth wyne it Sleaethe the fevor
and is very good for a Cancer. And it is comfortable for the
healinge of wound and other diseases. It comfortethe the
Stomake and makethe good digestion

Anet.

Anet is an hearbe called Dill. The properties

It maketh good vryne, and asswagethe evill wynd in the
bodye, and destroyeth the yup. The Seede therof burnt
and strowed vppon a wound will heale it in short tyme.

And yf a man be Scalded on his members by a woman,
laye that poulder to them and it will heale him. Alsoe make
a Plaister wth Honey mixed with the same poulder and laye
it to the Emeroid and it will heale them forthewth.
It is hott and drye./

Anisium. Arnicetum

Anisium is an hearbe called Annyse. The Properties
It vnbyndeth and breakethe vnkynd fleame that engendrethe
in the Lung and putethe evill wynd out of the boddye. It open-
nethe the Stopinge of the Liver and Mylt. It encreasethe
mylke in weomens breast. And it provokethe man or
woman to the worke of Nature. It makethe good vryne.
and temperethe the wombe. t is laxative. and makethe one
to Sweate. Alsoe drinke three Spoon full of the iuyce therof
when you have an evill Stomake and it will open it and cast
out the Corruption therof by vomitt. Alsoe take the seede &
Stampe it and drinke it and it is good for him that hath a payne
in his Codd.This hearbe is called sweete Cummen. It
may be keept in vertue three yeares. It is hott in the third
degree.






Apium Rijsus.

Lege Pliniu
Et Annotationes
D. Hollandi in
Marginem lib. 20
cap. ii dicitr
Apiastrum la:
Ranunculus.
1. Crowefoote
called flamula
our Speerewort
or Apium Risus.

Apium Rysus is an hearbe called Chervile or herba
Scelerata. It hath Small leaves lieke Humlock
but they be sweete in Savor and bearethe a small white
flowre with longe Seed lieke otes. He that bearethe
this about him ther shull noe Iugler doe him harme
and he that drinketh it wth wyne, shall make water
very well. And it ceasethe the ache in the Raynes and
in the bladder. and dothe vnbynd evill wynd from
the sides, the Stoppinge of the wombe and of the Liver
And it helpethe to heale wound. And wthholdeth Castinge
Alsoe a plaster of this hearbe tempered wth Aysell
destroyethe wyld fyer and healethe the Canker. This
hearbe is one of the Smallages and is called Apium
Rijsus Crowfoote or Ache It groweth on Sandye grownd
or on gravelie land. This hearbe brayed in a Morter
wth newe Swynes donge made in a Plaster and
layed to a botche will breake it in short tyme And it
may be gathered all tymes of the yeare.

Apium Domesticum

Apium Domesticum the second Smallage or Stonmarche
or Marche It is common & hath a stronge Savor: It maketh
good vryne. It openeth the Stopinge of the Liver. The
Seede therof dronke make the hard a womans wombe. and
the propertie is. It will drawe all evill humors in a mans
boddye to the Stomake & the wombe. And it is forbidden
to weomen wth Child. and them t have the fallinge Sicknes
Alsoe it ingendrethe the Pestilence and therfore it is dange-
rous for weomen t have sucking Children to meddle wth it.
for feare of madnes or the fallinge Sicknes But it is good
for Ache and Bruses. It is hott & drye. ffor the Stranguarie
Take the Iuyce of Smallage, Saxifrage & Phillipendula Seethe them
togither & Straine them and put to a little Sugar and
drinke it and it will cause one to make water presently. pved.

Apium Silvaticum

Apium Silvaticum is an hearbe called Serfoyle it groweth
lieke woodbyne and hath a white flowre lieke a Lyllie but they
be hangeinge. the properties
Stampe & temper it wth Honey and it is good for the Cancer in the
Mouthe. Drinke it wth wyne and it healethe the akeinge in the wombe
Alsoe Seethe it in wyne or water and washe the head therwth and
it helpethe the evill called the Vertyne. Alsoe frye it wth Oyle
Oliffe and freshe Sheepes Tallowe and it is good for sore Syn-
newes and Lymbes that be taken wth cold And some saye t
Chervile dothe the Same. It is hott and drye in the third degree.

Apium Ranarum.

Apium Ranarum is water Crowefoote. It hath yeallowe flowers
It hath a stalk of a Cubit highe wth many branches & growethe
in Watrye places. It is one of the Smallages. Laye it in wyne
and oyle till it be rotten then straine it and sett it over the fyre
with wexe and make therof an Oyntment wch dothe give greate
comfort to the Spleane that is grieved by Melancholie humors,
by reason of abundance wherof heavines comethe.

Apium Emerodarum.

Apium Emerodarum land Crowefoote it hath leaves
lieke Rames foote and bearethe a yeallowe fflower. It hath a longe
Stalke and growethw in Meddowes and moist places. This
hearbe sodden in wyne is good to laye vnto the Emerodes
and the Pyles when they bleade or be Swolne and the pouder
therof is good for the same.





Arnoglossa.

Arnoglossa is a common hearbe called Planten or waybreade

The vertues and properties.

If one have the head ake let him take e roote therof and hange it
& hange it about his neck and it will helpe him in Short tyme.
And if one hathe an akeinge in his wombe lett him frincke
the iuyce therof and aswage the payne. Alsoe if
a man hath a Swellinge in any part of him let him
Stampe this hearbe & laye to it & it will asswage it. The
iuyce therof dronle comfortethe the brest & helpethe all
diseases in the bodye. Alsoe Stampe the Seede hereof to pouder
& strowe it vppon a wound and it will aswage the heate &
burneinge therof. Alsoe the Iuyce temped wth as much
vinegre & dronke Stoppethe the fluxe and tempeth the
the wombe. And drincke the Iuyce therof with wyne, and it
destroyethe e venome & byteinge of Serpentes & adders
& madd dogg. and destroyethe wormes in the bodye if
it be stamped and layd to the Navill. Alsoe take the Iuyce
herof & temper it wth the glare of an Egge cleane puryfied
& wett cleane flaxe therin & laye it as a Plaster to Sore eyes
and it will heale it. and for Swellinge and ryseinge in e
mouthe take e Iuyce & hold it in e mouthe & it will asswage the
Swellinge. Stampe it with honey & make a plaster therof &
it clensethe and healethe wett wound. Alsoe Seethe it and make
wort and eate them and it healethe the dropsey. the Iuyce therof
dronke healethe the fallinge evill the frensey and the Canker &
helpethe the heate and Swellinge of the eyes. and clenseth e
fleshe about e gummes and putethe awaye the Toathe ache.
This herbe and Tansey sodden in wyne and dronke Staunchethe the
Menstruous flux. The Seedes therof dronke are good for Sores
in the bladder and for the Raines of the backe. the Root therof borne
about one necke will free him from Tetters. The lesse Planten that is called
Ribworte will doe all these thing aforesaid. And the iuyce therof
wth wyne given to a woman wth child to drinke will make reddie deliverance
of the child & of e second burthen. alsoe e Iuyce dronke Stoppethe the bleedinge at e
nose. The Iuyce mixed wth vinegre honey and poulder of Allom healethe canker
in e mouthe. The Iuyce mixed wth e Iuyce of lettuce & Aysell wth a little wheate
flower made in plaister, & laid to e right side healeth e Iaundise if it be vsed. cum multis

Arthemesia

Arthemesia is Mugworte. It is lieke Wormewoode but is more
greene. the Vertue and properie therof.

If a woman drincke the Iuyce therof in the tyme of hir flowres wth
wyne it will cause hir to Conceyve a Child. and causethe a wo-
man to be delivered of a Child Or put in hir Secret it healpethe the
mother of swellinge and hardnes. It is alsoe good for the Iaundise
and makethe one well coulored. Alsoe make pouder therof and of hore-
hownd and strewe it vppon the teat of the Emerod and it will
heale them if they be first annoynted wth freshe grease. Alsoe
make a plaister of Mugworte and laye it cold to the Navell of a wo-
man t is wth a dead Child and it will make hir to be delivered.
The Iuyce therof dronke healethe the Stone in the Raynes of the backe
and makethe one to make water well. And if one goe a iourney let
him beare this hearbe with him in his Shoes and he shall not be fainte
nor weary of the iourney. Alsoe annoynte the Soles of yo feete at yo
goeinge forthe and it comfortethe greatelye. And Stampe it wth freshe
grease & it helpethe Chafinge, Itchinge and Clawinge of hand legg
& other lymbes. If this hearbe be keept in a howse ther shall noe
wicked Spiritt have power to doe harme or abyde wthin the
howse. This hearbe dronke in Must is good for many evill: for it
comfortethe the harte, the Stomake and all inner part of the
bodye in many diseases. Alsoe, make poulder therof and drinke
it wth luke warme water & it is good for byteinge of venemous
beast. Whosoe annoyntethe his bodye therwth and oyle oliffe
every daye shall have noe ffevor nor Shakeing hand. Laye
this hearbe vnder a sicke mans head that cannot sleepe nor
take noe rest he is liekelye to live yf he take rest, but if he can
take noe rest it is liekelye he will dye. It is hott and drye in
the third degree. The pouder of it layde vppon the pyles
healethe them.





Astula Regia.
Astula Regia is an hearbe called Woodroff It is lieke
a weede called Cratche weede or Chickens meate and
soe it savorethe when it is greene and when it is drye
it is very sweete. If one have a disease in his
mouthe let him boyle this hearbe in wyne and washe ye
mouthe therwth and the patient shalbe healed.

Astrologia Longa.
Astrologia Longa is an hearbe called Madder. It hathe leaves
lieke to ye Mader Warants and is one of the Spices of
Mader And hath branches ye lengthe of a Cubitt wth
many small branches comeinge out of the Roote, and the
branches have many small knotts and bearethe somwhat
a white flowre. If it be dronke wth wyne it will unbynd
the stopinge of the Liver and will wynde that are stopped
wh'in the bodye. Alsoe this hearbe clensethe wounds that patry:
fie and wax rotten. drinke it wth wyne and it will purge
& helpe all manner of Venome yt restethe wh'in the bodye.
Alsoe the Juyce therof & the Juyce of Gladen and Honey
mixed well togither and annoynte therwth the Gummes &
Chawes & it will take awaye all the blacknesse and fylthe
of the teethe. And drinke the Juyce therof wth Warme
water and it helpethe the Splen, the Fevors the Crampe
the Podogra Morbo Caduro, the Sore of the wombe and
the Palsey. The Smoke therof drivethe evill Spirits out
of the howse and comfortethe Children and helpethe the
Hickhock. Some men call this hearbe patience.
It is drye in the first degree and hott in the second.
It is attractive of Colorique Complexion and of
Orientall kynd Maskalyne.





Astrologia Rotunda.
Astrologia Rotunda is an hearbe yt some men call
Gallingale. The leaves therof are somethinge lieke to the leaves
of Barr save yt they be greene about and white underneathe
and hath but one branche. The vertue.
Ipocrates saythe, if it be dronke wth warme water it is
medicynable for those yt are stopped in the brest, and for ye
fallinge Sicknes the Podagra and the Crampe.
It florishethe as it were Bitternie but the flower is white
and must be gathered in harvest. And it is one of the
Spices of redd Madder and is hott and drye of the nature of
the fyre. It is best in Physique, the rootes are better then the
leaves if it be gathered before it bare flowres. The pouder
hereof and the Juyce of Mynts waste the dead fleshe. And make
a Tent wette wth honey and this pouder, and it healethe the
Fistula and Smartinge Scabbe beinge cast uppon them.

Asterion
Asterion is an hearbe called Lunaria. It growethe in highe
places amonge stones. It Spineth by night. It is found most
by Sheepherds in the fields. It bearethe a yeallow flowre hole
and round lieke a Cocke bell or lieke the flowre of Foxe gloves.
The leaves of this hearbe be of an Indian blewe and have the marke
of the Moone in the myddest of the leafe, as it were three leaved
grasse rownde as a pennye. This hearbe hath a Redd stalke and
smellethe lieke Muske and the juyce therof is yeallowe and
it growethe in the newe of the Moone wthout leaves, and everye
daye it encreasethe a leafe till the Moone be att the full, and
everie daye after it loosethe a leafe till the moone be at the
Wayne. It growthe in greate quantitie wher it growethe.
Whosoe eatethe of this hearbe after the full of ye Moone when
the Signe is in Virgo It wil helpe him of the Fallinge Sick=
nesse. Alsoe if he beare it about his neck it will doe the same
and helpethe him wthout Doubt. /





Auricula Maris.
Auricula Maris is an hearbe called Mouseare. It hathe
small leaves lieke those of Gratia dei, greene about &
white beneathe wth a yeallowe flowre, and it gorwthe
creepeninge uppon the ground wthout a stalke. The Juyce
therof is lieke Mylke and the leafe hearie lieke a Mouses
Eare. If you drinke the juyce it healethe the Quinsey
And ye Juyce therof is good to heale all kynd of wounds.
Alsoe the juyce dronke wth wyne and stronge Ale, and
annoynte the Raynes wth the blodd of a Foxe, and it is
Soveraigne medysyne for the Stone. And take the
juyce therof and the juyce of Millefollie and drincke it
wth white wyne and it will cause one to make water freshlye.
Alsoe if one that is Sicke drincke the juyce if he keepe
it he shall live, and if he doe not it is token of deathe.

Arthemisia media.
Arthemisia media the Myddle Mugworte. It helpethe
the Strangurie & the Payne in the bladder. Alsoe take halfe
a pynte of wyne & put therto a dragme of Mugworte –
Tagantes and give it to him that hath the fever, wth warme
water and it will helpe him. Bruise it wth Vinegre and
Laye it plaisterwise to the Sore Brest or sore sied and it
helpethe wh'in three dayes. And for payne in the Synnewes
seethe this hearbe in meate oyle and laye it to the griefe.

Arthemisia Mimma.
Arthemisia Mimma the least Mugworte is called
Matricaria & hath a Savor lieke Elder when it is bruised.
Braye Mugworte while it is greene wth Oyle of Almonds
& make a plaister therof and laye it warme to the brest and
it will make it whole wthin five dayes. And laye it under the
Threshold of the howse & ther shull nobodye have power to
doe hurte wthin yt howse. Alsoe the juyce therof meddled
wth oyle of Roses annoynted warme on sore Synnewes or
Shrinkinge





or Shrinkeinge Synnewes or Shakeinge hands and wastethe the
payne: It is said that the Goddesse Diana did fynd these three
Mugworts & their Vertues and she gave them to Centaurus
wch proved the Vertues of them diverse tymes. And therfore
Diana is named Arthemisia. It must be gathered in May
and July./

Atriplex.
Atriplex is an hearbe called Arache or Orache it hathe vertue
to Loosen ye wombe It molefiethe all harnes and healethe
the opilations caused of the said hardnes. This herbe put
in plaister healeth the Pearle in the Eye and clearethe the
sight and Chopped and layed in Apostemes it refreshethe
and Coolethe them. The Seede is good for them that have
the Jaundise by opilation or stoppinge of the Liver.
The drinke that Orache is sodden in is good for the
Jaundise. The water that the Seede is sodden in wth the
Roote of Rapes and a little Vineger dronke in greate
quantitie purgethe the Stomake of Fleame and Choler
and is good agaynst a disease called Sacerignis or Hell
Fyre beinge stamped and applied therto. And it will
helpe the Pogagra or Gowte & the Swellinge of the feete
if it be brayed wth honey and Vinegre and applyed.
it is cold in the first degree, and moist in the Second./

Asarabacca.
Asarabacca hathe leaves much lieke to a Violett leafe but that
it is a more darke greene. Drincke ye wyne or ye Water that
this hearbe is sodden in and it provokethe uryne and flowres
in woomen and it is good agaynst the opilaton of the Liver
caused of cold matter. It is good for ye Mylte the dropsey
Siatica passio and the Matrice It yealdethe good Coler to those
that have ye Ioteries or the Jaundices. It purgethe the wombe
It breakethe fleame and causethe Vomitt. And is almost as
vehement to pvoke Vomitt as white Eleber or Peletorie
but it is not soe stronge. And therfore the naturall
strengthe





Strengthe of the patient ought to be considered that it
shalbe mynistred unto and of what complexion he be
whether he be leane or fatt, for it is more given unto
fatt men then unto leane. And thus it ought to be given.
Take Thirtie leaves of Asarabacca and laye them in
Wyne all night and in the morninge beate them well
& give them to drincke wth the same wyne, or else
in the tyme of ye yeare seathe them wth fatt Porke and
give it to the patient to eate, and after drinke stronge wyne.
if you will. Thirtie leaves is to be given to the strongest
complexion and to other after the strengthe of their nature
And when it is given in wrightinge in this wise Asarabac=
caria then it is mente ye rootes and not the leaves.
but when the leaves, they be expreslie named.
It is hott and drye in the third degree.

Avena.
Avena is a grayne called Otes. It hath lieke vertue
as Barley. Take Otemeale grots cleane fryed and
beate them in a Morter and put warme water therto by
little and little, then straine it thoroughe a cleane
strainer, and seethe it till it be thick: then put therto
a good quantitie of Almond mylke and Sugar and
this is very comfortable for them that have the hott
sore Ague. And it is a Sovereigne thinge to rypen
an Aposteme. It is cold and moist in the third degree./





Balsamum/
Balsamum is an hearbe called Bawnie or Melissa. The vertue
Steepe it in wyne till it be thoroughe wett and make water therof
by distillation and drinke a Spoonefull everye morninge fasting
and it norishethe the brayne and refreshethe the wilts and mak=
ethe one to have reddye remembrance of any thinge he hath
done or shall doe. Alsoe it is good for a cold Stomake, and
maketh ne to have a light hearte, and keepethe him from
heavines. It strengthenethe the Vaynes and Synnewes
It will helpe them wch have lost their Speache. It helpethe
the palsey. And if one hath hurte his Tonge let him wett a
blewe clothe in this water and laye it theron and it will
heale it in shot tyme, and it is good for all other diseases in
the Mouthe. Alsoe it is good for Sause fleame faces, for it
makethe pure fayre visages. It causethe a woman to conceyve
and helpethe all diseases of weomen. And for the fallinge
sicknes take the juyce therof and drincke it wth a little Treacle
fastinge and it will helpe in short tyme. It healethe the goute
the Aposteme, the palsey the efvor quarten, and it breake=
ethe the stone and makethe it to avoyde wthout any
payne. It avoydethe poyson and healethe the sore in
the bladder. If you Sprinkle of this water uppon
Rawe fleshe ther will noe corruption come therto
And if a vessell of wyne be changed put of this water
into it, and it will come agayne. Alsoe the juyce mixte
wth Oyle Oliffe and the head that akethe annoynted therwth
it will put awaye ye ache and payne. The Juyce dronke
bringethe forthe the second burden of a woman and medell
it wth vinegre & oyle and it helpethe the Podagra from
swellinge and mekethe it whole. This herbe sodden in
water is good to washe him that is Skabby or sore of bravell.





Basilica

Basilica is an hearbe called Basill. Ther are three
kynd one hath leaves much lieke Rewe wth flowres
& Savor of ffrankencense, an other hathe a blewe flowre
and the third is called Garifolaton, The vertue of that
wth the blewe flowre is. Eate it fastinge in the Morninge
and it will make a glad hearte & put a quakeinge and
Suspition. it healethe the burneing Choler. It helpethe a
Cold Stomake and Comfortethe the brayne. It makethe
one to sleepe well. Alsoe for swellinge of Sore eyes.
The Garifflaton is strongest in medecyne, and he com-
fortethe the Stomake and makethe good digestion. It is
good for the Cardiacle and the black Choler and for
fleame. Alsoe put this hearbe into the Nostrill and
it will vnbynde the lowe bondes of the brayne. And if
one swell of bloodlettinge braye it and laye it to it &c.
It is good. for the eyes.

Balsmita

Balsmita is an herbe called Horsemynt and is
common. Some men call it water Mynt for it growethe
muche in water & savoreth ranker then other Mynt
It comfortethe the Stomake, makethe good digestion.
purgethe well the bodye and makethe one to sleepe
well The Iuyce therof medled withnwyne and honey
is a good drinke to put Coldnes out of the Stomake
& evill wynd. Seethe e Iuyce in wyne and give
it to a woman that travelethe wth Child and shee
shalbe easelye delivered. ther be two Spices of them.
It is hott and drye.






80
Barba Iovis

Barba Iovis is an Hearbe called Iuberb or howselieke.
If one have an Aposteme within him take this hearbe in the
beginninge of the said Aposteme wth redd Coriander and
Tallowe. Stampe all these togither in manner of a plaister
and laye it to the Aposteme and destroye it And of one be
burnt with fyre or Skalede wth hott liqour: Take the iuyce
of this hearbe Oyle of Roses and waxe and make an oyntmt
therof and annoynte the Sore but the first three dayes after
it is hurt and it will helpe it. Alsoe the Iuyce is good to helpe
all wicked heat, Swellinges and hardnes. The Iuyce
Stamped with Crames of white breade Coriander and Swynes
grease helpeth all Swelling Sicknes. It is cold and moiste.

Baucia

Baucia is an hearbe called Skirwitt or Pastinaca. Ther be
Two kynd of them. It is good for them t have bene lateleie
Sicke and for Melancholick persones if they eate them Rawe
or sodden. It is better greene then drye. Ther is a confection
made of this hearbe t encouragethe a mans lust of the
world and causethe good digestion. Seethe the root in the cleare-
rest runninge water, and when they have sodden a good
while cutt them in small peeces and fashon them rounde &
longe, then straine the water and seethe them agayne with
honey till they be thick as honey, and stirr them well that
they cleave not to the pann, and in the myddest of their
seethinge put therto blanched Almond, the seed of pyne
aples blanched, Ginger Cynamme, Gallingall Pepper,
Maces & Nuttmeg and put them altogether and stirr them
till they be well. And eate therof when you will for it
greatlye comfortethe.





Bardana

Bardana is an hearbe called Lappa Maior. It is e
Clott or Burres that growe on fatt grownd. the Roote stamped
wth a little Salt healethe the byteinge of a Madd Dogge.
It helpethe the hott Ague. And for stinkeinge wound
washe them with the water that it is sodden in, and make
an oyntemt therof, mingled wth a little Salvister and
greece picle and vinegre and laye it therto. The Iuyce
of the leaves dronke easeth the payne in the bellie.

Beta

Beta is called Beetus. Some saye it swellethe the head,
and Gallen saithe it is not good for them that have much
Gall but he saythe it is good and holsome for those that
be flegmatique and he affirmethe that it is holsome
for the Stomake and makethe a good color He that
vsethe it with vinegre shall have good talent to his meate
It neshethe the Stomake. The Iuyce of it and hownd-
bitt, or the water stilled and mixed togither, and honey
& Eysell dronke helpethe him t hath lost his Speache
sodenlie. The iuyce therof put into ones Nostrill
dothe put awaye all evill humours out of the head.
Eate it or drinke it & laye it to the byles and it will
helpe them. The Iuyce therof fastenethe the heare
of the head if it be washed therwth. drinke it with
wyne and it makethe one to sleepe well and maketh
a Nurse to have store of myke. It ceasethe the flixe
It comfortethe e sight and putethe awaye the swellinge
of heate. And Diascorides saithe ther are two Spices
therof: the one blacke and the other white. It is hott
in e first degree and moyste in the seconde degree.





81
Betonia

Betonia Bittenie or Bishopes worte. Stampe
this hearbe and laye it to a wound in the head and it will heale
it shortlye. if it be refreshed everie third daye it will heale
the better and drawe out broken bones of e wound. Alsoe
meddle the Iuyce of Bittenie with warme water and drinke
it fastinge and it will break the webb in the eye, and clense
e sight, cease the wateringe of e eyes and make them cleare
if it be applied. And if you have Ache in yo eares, take e
Iuyce of Bittenie and a little Rose water and warme it and
put it into the Eare and stoppe it with wooll and it will make
you whole. And if one bleede at the nose take Bittenie and
Salt and Chafe them well togither and stopp the
nostrill therwith that it fall not out and it will stanche
Presentlie. Take Bittonie and Seethe it in wyne or
Vinegre till the third part be wasted, then hold it in your
mouthe till it be cold, and it will helpe the Tootheache .
ffor the Coughe take two ounces of the pouder of Bittenie and
Seethe it wth honey over an easie fire and eate therof nyne
dayes. And if a mann cannot digest his meate take
iiij. ounces of this pouder and medle it wth clarified honey
and drinke therof and then make small pellett of the said
pouder and honey as much as an hasell nutt, and eate everie
daye three of them. and drinke everie daye of the medecyne
wth wyne iij. Spoonefulles and it will help.Alsoe if a mans
yard be Swolne or sore stampe Bettenie and temper it wth
wyne or vinegre and laye it therto. Take Bittenie
and Seethe it in warme water yill the third part be wasted,
then take out the hearbe and stampe it, and laye it warme
to the feete for the Podagra. Alsoe take every daye a
quantitie of Bittenie or the pouder therof fastinge and
that daye you shall not be dronke. This pouder eaten wth
honey fastinge is good for the Stomake, the Coughe, the
Tisect & many other diseases. It is hott and drye in
the third degree.





Bicula

Bicula is an hearbe called Bugell ther be two Spices
therof. The vertue of the first. Stampe it, and drinke
it with wyne and it clenseth the Redd Choler and purge the
the burneinge of the Stomake & of the harte and it
clensethe the blacke Choler that is called Cardiuea
and helpethe evill humors of the lung. Alsoe
the Iuyce Mingled wth wyne if it be cast amonge
people at a feaste it will cause them to be glad and
merrie

The second Bugell hathe leaves somwhat rounde
of a sadd greene. his flowers blewe. It will heale
wound in a mans heade if it be Stamed and
drunke wth wyne. It Strengtheneth the broken bones
in the head. And they are bothe hotte & drye.

Bearefoote.

Bearefoote hath vertue to soften and Rype. Seethe the hearbe in
Bores grease and laye it to Apostemes of the privie Members
and it will heale them. Alsoe for payne of the Mylte and drynes
of Synnewes that be Shronke. Take Barefoote and Bittenie
and laye them longe in Oyle Oliffe and sett them on the
fyre and put therto Virgin Waxe and make an Oyntment
therof and it will helpe. In this oytement the leaves are
to be vsed whiles they be greene. It is hott & moyste in e first degree

Berula

Berula is an hearbe called Beler. It hath leaves lieke
Skirwitt and growethe in waters and hath a longe stalke
The virtue of this hearbe is verye pretious to rype Sores
Byles & Botches. for it is hott and drye.





82
Burago Domestica

Burago Domestica Is an hearbe called Borage. It clensethe
the Red Choler and it is good for the Cardiacle and apostemes
that doe engender of blacke Choler. Borage put in wyne
makethe them merye that drinke therof. Dicit Borago
gaudia Semper ago. and that daye you drinke therof you shall
not be poysoned. Alsoe braye the roote of Borage and temp
it wth a little stale Ale and Saffron and it healethe the Iaundise
for this herbe is hott and moist in the first degree.

Borago Silvestris

Borago Silvestris is wyld Borage or Calleworte. this hearbe
hathe greate leaves and eben longe not soe broad as other Borage
and hath a blew flowre wth a greate roote. it groweth on drye
growndes and on waye sides.

Bugale

Bugula is an Hearbe called Brome Bugell, and some call it
Self heale Thevertue therof. It will heale a wound in
any part of a mans bodie wthout helpe of other thing. It
breakethe and destroyethe the Rhume in ones head and the
Stopinge and akeinge, ffor it is hott and drye and ther be two
Spyces therof.

Brokelempe.

Brokelempe is an Hearbe much lieke Mynt. and if it be brayed
and meddled wth Sheepes Tallowe and made hott in water or in
manner of a Plaister snd layd to any manner of Swellinge
It will in short tyme asswage it.





Barnetta.
Barnetta Burnett hathe leaves lieke Tansey and blewe
flowres. It hath virtue to destroye grosse humors and to cease
and staye the Laxe: It openethe the Stoppinge of the Liver
and it makethe good uryne. Drinke it wth wyne and it helpethe
the Jaundise. The juyce of this herbe and honey dronke togither
unbyndethe the Sore under the rybbes and sore Pappes. Therbe
two Spices of this hearbe one growethe in gardens and hard
ground & the other in Meddowes and other places.

Bursa Pastoris.
Bursa Pastoris is Sheepherds purse. It is to be gathered
in June in the wayneinge of the Moone. This hearbe
Stanchethe blood at the nose if the patient beare it in his right
hand greeene or drye. Alsoe the pouder therof dronke wth wyne
helpethe them that are brused or have the laske. And if a veyne
be Cutt drinke the Juyce of it and it will stanche the blood. It
healeth wounds and keepethe them from Softninge. It is hott & drye.

Brisma.
Brisma is called Cucurbita agrestis or wyld Slepp It
growethe in hedges and hathe agreate roote Ther are two kynds
therof, the black and the white. That wch beare the redd seede
is called the black and the other hath a white seede. The black is
the best. It is good agaynst Spasma. that is a certayne
Crampe or goute. If ye roote be hanged about the patients
nack it will helpe him shortley. The Juyce dronke three
or fowre tymes helpethe them that Spett blood. Drinke the Juyce
therof wth vinegre and that weeke you shall not be dronke.
Alsoe drinke the broathe that it is sodden in wth honey and it will
take awaye the payne of Collica paslo & Ilica passio. Alsoe
for the kings evill Canker or other Sores: take ye roote of Briomie &
Astrologia in lieke quantitie and beate them wth honey and grease and
laye it therto. Alsoe for them that have broken bones in their hands
or other places: Take xli Croppes of Briony and three ounces
of glasse broken and boyled togither in iii Cyathes of wyne
till the





till the two parts be wasted, then straine it and give it the patiente
Hypocrates saythe That a man had all the Fingers of his hands
eaten and knawne wth Sicknes, and he used this medecyne and
made him whole. Alsoe take the leaves and seeds of Bryonie and
burne them to asshes and meddle them wth the Fayre of the same
hearbe & make an Oyntment and therwth annoynte warts and
they will fall of: The Juyce holden longe in the mouthe healethe
the Cankered Gummes. And Seathe this hearbe in fayre runninge
water and therwth washe a womans sore pappes that the mylke is
corrupted and Crudded in and it will mortifie and case the payne.
Alsoe take the Seeds that growe fowre togither and it helpethe
the Fevor Quarten if it be dronke before it come. Alsoe the water
that the budds be sodden in pvoke one to make good uryne, and
makethe a woman that lakethe mylke to give milke plentifullye.

Baglossa.
Baglosse is an hearbe called Lange de beofe or wyld Borage. The leaves are full
of pricks and bearethe a yeallowe flowre, and it must be gathered in June and
July. The roote sodden in water is good for them yt have the fevor Quartto and especyally
the Buglosse yt hath fowre stalks groweinge on one roote wth seedes Seethe
the roots therof and give it the sick to drincke. Alsoe Stampe this hearbe
wth honey and Cromes of bread, and make a plaister therof and laye it to an
Aposteme and it will breake it and purge it. Alsoe the juyce dronke wth
honey comfortethe the weakenes of the hearte, and lettethe sigheninge. The
water yt Buglosse hath bene sodden in helpethe agaynst abundance of redd
Choler, the drye Choler & the blacke choler. It Comfortethe and establishethe the mynd if man.
And if it be eaten wth Lettuce it maketh good Temperature and give blood for ye
coldnes of Lettuce temperethe ye greate heate & keepe the well Chastitie and
for Consolation of Companie take the water of Buglosse hath bene sodden
in and Sprinkle it about the howse or place, wher Companie is togither and it
will make them all reiayne and be merrye. Ther are three kynds of this herbs
The first bearethe a flowre blewe of a Skye Couler. The second bearethe
a white flowre. the rots blacke wthout and white wthin. and hathe greate
stalkes lieke Catts tayles. The third hath a yeallow flowre wth many leaves
full of pricks & therfore some call it Aspergo or Asparell. But that
wch bearethe the blew flowre is best in medecyne. It is hott and moist
in the first degree. /





Calendula.
Calendula is an hearbe called Marygowld. Solsequium.
Helitropium, Solmaria, Sponsa Solis. Some call it Calendula
because it bearethe flowres all tymes of the yeare. The
Juyce therof dronke or eate wth a reare Egg provokethe
weomens flowres and comfortethe the Stomake. Alsoe the
Juyce therof put into ye Nostrills of one that is payned
wth the tootheache will ease him. And if one be bitten
of any Venomouse beast eate this hearbe and it will heale
him. The Juyce therof dronke helpethe the stoppinge of
the Liver. And it hath the vertue that Endiffe hathe.

Camelion
Camelion is an hearbe called Wolfe Thistle. It bearethe
a redd flowre the leaves are sharpe. The vertue of this
hearbe is this gather it when is the Sonne is in Capricorne
and in the newe of the moone and that daye you beare
it wth you noe evill thinge shall hurt you. /

Canabria
Canabria wyld Hempe, Holy Rope or dum nettle
the leaves be llieke Hempe. Rubbe them uppon the
pulces of him that hath the fevor and it helpethe him
It growethe by waters:

Caprisolium
Caprisolium is Woodbyne. The vertue is: to heale wounds
Cankers and Blisters, Akeinge of Teethe and Sore
in the Tonge. And it helpethe agaynst stinginge of
Bees & Waspes. And Stampe it and laye it to wounds
and twill drawe out the blood. It is hott and drye.





Capillus Veneris
Capillus Veneris is called Mayden haire or water worte.
It hath leaves lieke ferne but they are smaller. It groweth
muche in walls and Stones, and in the middest of the leafe
ther is a stringe lieke blacke haire. If it be dronke wth
wyne it will destroye and breake the Stone and destroyethe
all manner of venome. /

Canapis
Canapis is Hempe ther are two kynds of them the Common
& the wylde. The wyld is hott and drye in the second degree
for payne and Swellinge in the brests meddle this hearbe
wth grease and as a plaister laye it to the Sore and it will
cease the ache and the Swellinge And it rypenethe all
Apostemes and breakethe them. The Juyce of the Roote
meddled wth Capons grease and vinegre annoynted helpethe
the Pose, the goute, that comethe of Cold and if it be
meddled wth nettle seede it wastethe cold Impostemes and
breakethe them. /

Cardiaca
Cardiaca as an hearbe called Vylesper it hathe leaves
lieke the wyte nettle and it will pricke somethinge uppon ye
Tonge and it bearethe a little seede in small Codds.
If it be eate or dronke it is good for the fallinge Evill

Eidanum Earthe nutt.
Earthe nutt or Eidanum is an hearbe that hathe leaves
lieke fennell wth flowres and small stalks and growethe
in woods and meddowes. And if it be Stamped and layed
to a sore wound it will fetche out all the dead fleshe out of
the sore. Alsoe make a plaister therof and laye it to a place
that lackethe haire and it will restore it agayne./





Carduus Benedictus.

Carduus Benedictus called St Marye Thistle it bearethe
a broad leafe full of white strik and in the topps of the
stalk bearethe a greate burr full of sharpe prick. Hange
it in a howse xiiij dayes till it be drye. Then laye it vppon
a fayre Clothe in the sonne and when it is thoroughe drye
beate it to pouder. And if one feele him self grieved wth
a prickinge of the pestelence take the same pouder wthin
xoj howres after the saod priclinge beginethe the quan-
titie of a walnutt or more, and as muche Treacle and a spoone-
full of the water of Dragons and mingle them well togither
and take it vpp wth the poynte of a knife and eate it wthin
xij howres and it will cause e sicknes to breake out in diverse
places; and this medecyne is as pretious for poyson. Keepe
well this pouder for it is worthe gold and hathe bene well
proved.

Carawaye.

Carawaye is a gentle hearbe and Comin looseth his
strengthe wher Carawaye is psente. If it be stamped
and dronke wth stronge Ale it will destroye all evill
wynd in  the bowell. It puttethe awaye the Coughe, and
healethe them wch are infected wth frensie, or bitten with venemous
wormes or beastes. This hearbe medled wth vinegre healethe Scabbes.
and makethe haire to growe in bald places or head if they be annoyn-
ted therwth. And drinke it with stale Ale and it healeth the
menstrewes and swellinge of the flank. Eate it or drinke
it and it causeth good digestion. It Sleaethe wormes in the
bowell. It helpethe castinge that comethe of flesume. It
puttethe out evill wynd of the bodye. It moystnethe the Stomake
and makethe good vryne, And if it be eaten it destroyethe itche,
and Scabbes It comfortethe the wynd and clearethe the voyce.
It is hott and drye. Dum Carawaye carui tunc sine febre fui.

Cassula maior. Orpyn./

Cassula maior is called Orpyn It bearethe a Crymson flowre vppon
one Stalke The vertue of it is. If it be Stamped and layed to a wound
it will heale it without helpe of any other thinge.

Cassula minor.

Cassula minor is an hearbe called Stonecropp. It groweth lowe, and
sometyme vppon moiste walles and howses. the vertue of it is If
it be dronke with beere wyne or Ale it keepethe him from lecherie
that drinkethe it.

Calus Domesticus

Calus Domesticus is Cole or Caboche. The vertue therof is it will
heale all freshe and old wound Alsoe washe the Canker with hott wyne
and after Stampe this hearbe with wyne and laye it therto, and it
will cleare the Canker. Alsoe take Barlie meale and mingle
it wth the iuyce of Cole, Hearbe Grace and Coriander, well stamped
togither and it will heale the Podagra, the sore Toes that be
chaffed with goeinge and Swellinge. Alsoe the vryne of him
that vsethe to eate Caule is a Soverainge thinge to washe Synewes
that be dryed and shronke. and the Children that be washed wth
this vryne it will keepe them in good healthe. And he that vseth
te eate Cole it preservethe his sight cleare. And it makethe a
Nurse have plentie of mylke. It clensethe the menstruewes
and castethe out e dead Child out of a woman. And it is comfortable
to the Stomake. And if it be softlye sodden it mollifiethe the wombe
and if it be muche sodden it stoppethe the wombe. Alsoe Stampe
Cole wth Allom & vinegre and it healethe the liver and it maketh
haire to growe wher it is beare. and it helpethe the Swellinge in the
privie members, and muche the better if it be sodden wth Beanes. Alsoe
Seethe Caule and fenegreeke togither in vinegre, Straine it and
drinke it, and it helpethe the streines of Synewes & e Podagra if you
make a plaister of e hearbe and laye it therto. Alsoe burne the stalk
of Caule & e Ashes therof medled with old grease made in Oyntmt
healethe e payne in the rybbes & of the sides. And the seed mixed wth
vinegre helpethe Sores. And drinke it & it avoydethe wormes out of the bodye
Th Iuyce of Caule put into the Nostrill puttethe out fleame out of e
heade, and purgethe e brayne, e brest and e voyce. Alsoe the pithe of e
Stalk eaten keepe a man from dronkennes. Alsoe take vpp the
Roote and let it touche noe more the ground, and let him that hath freckles
beare some therof about his Necke and it will take them awaye.






Centaurus maior.

Centaurus Maior is e greater Centaurie or Earthe Gall It hath leaves
lieke the lesse Centaurie but t they be whiter. It bearethe yeallowe
flowres in the topp of the Stalke. If it be sodden in wyne &
dronke it healethe the sickness of the lyver and it drawethe evill
humors from the milke a thinge that hath bene well proved. The
water t  this hearbe is sodden in is good to washe woundes & to washe
a Canker. It growethe on drye grownd and on hill It is hott/

Centaurus Minor

Centaurie the lesse is hott and drye. If it be stamped and layed
to a wound it makethe it to goe togither that hath bene ill healed.
The water that it is sodden in, is good to washe Synnewes t be shronke.
The Iuyce dronke in wyne or Ale pvokethe flowres and castethe out
an abortive. Drinke the Iuyce in wyne and it helpethe the evill in
the wombe. Gather it in harvest drye it in the Sonne, make
pouder therof and drinke it wth stronge Ale or wyne, and it helpeth
them t have dronke venome, or be bitten wth any venomous beaste.

xttirundo
Chelidion.

Chelidonia is called Celendyne, ther be two kynd of them, and
bothe are good for the Eye sight. Plinie saythe t the younge
Swallows cannot see vntill e Dame dothe touche their eyes wth this
hearbe and therefore the Swallowe is called Chelidonia Celedyne wherof
this hearbe hathe his name. Take the iuyce of the flowres, and seethe
it wth honey in a cleane vessell till it be thick, and that is a
principall medecyne for the Eyes if they be often annoynted therwth.
Alsoe stampe e roote therof and temper it wth wyne or Aysell
and vse to drinke therof often, and it helpethe the Iundies.
and the Toothache & will kill wormes in the Teethe if the
roote be holden in the mouthe betwene the Teethe, and it ceasethe
the ache, and it helpethe the fistula; the Canker, the Collick,
and many other diseases. It is hott and dry in the fowrthe degree;
